116	  const st = w.store;
117	  for (let x = x1; x <= x2; x++) {
118	    for (let y = y1; y <= y2; y++) {
119	      if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) continue;
120	      if (st.isSolid(x, y)) return true;
121	    }
122	  }
123	  return false;

133	 * （触发时机教徒刚死、窗口在地表开阔带，与原版结果一致）。
134	 */
135	export function triggerLunarApocalypse(w: World, rng: RNG, host: LunarHost): void {
136	  const list = [TOWER_SOLAR, TOWER_VORTEX, TOWER_NEBULA, TOWER_STARDUST];
137	  const order: number[] = [];
138	  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
139	    order[i] = list[rng.int(0, list.length - 1)];   // Main.rand.Next(list.Count)
140	    list.splice(list.indexOf(order[i]), 1);
141	  }
142	  const colX = Math.floor(w.w / 5);                  // Main.maxTilesX / 5
143	  const surface = Math.floor(w.groundLevel);         // (int)Main.worldSurface
144	  for (let j = 0; j < 4; j++) {
145	    const num3 = colX * (1 + j);
146	    let placed = false;
147	    for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 30; attempt++) {
148	      const jitter = rng.int(-100, 100);             // Main.rand.Next(-100, 101)
149	      for (let y = surface; y > 100; y--) {
150	        if (!solidTiles(w, num3 + jitter - 10, num3 + jitter + 10, y - 20, y + 15)) {
151	          host.spawnTowerNpc(order[j], num3 + jitter, y);
152	          placed = true;
153	          break;
154	        }
155	      }
156	      if (placed) break;
157	    }
158	    if (!placed) host.spawnTowerNpc(order[j], num3, surface - 40);
159	  }
160	  const s = w.lunarEvent;
161	  s.towerActive.solar = s.towerActive.vortex = true;
162	  s.towerActive.nebula = s.towerActive.stardust = true;
163	  s.active = true;
164	  const max = shieldMax(w);
165	  s.shield.solar = s.shield.vortex = s.shield.nebula = s.shield.stardust = max;
166	  persist(w);
167	  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);                   // MessageLunarApocalypse（misc[43]）
168	}
169	
170	/** MessageLunarApocalypse（WorldGen.cs:87523-87546）：数已灭塔 n → Lang.misc[43+n]
171	 *  （43 天界入侵 / 44 头脑麻木 / 45 痛苦 / 46 阴森低语；47 需 n=4 此时 active 已 false 不可达） */
172	export function messageLunarApocalypse(w: World, host: LunarHost): void {
173	  if (!w.lunarEvent.active) return;
174	  let downed = 0;
175	  for (const k of TOWER_KEYS) if (!w.lunarEvent.towerActive[k]) downed++;
176	  host.broadcast(Lang.misc(43 + downed), 175, 75, 255);   // ChatColors.BossOrEvent
177	}
178	
179	/**
180	 * UpdateLunarApocalypse（WorldGen.cs:87438-87493）：场上无对应塔 NPC → TowerActive 置 false；
181	 * 四塔全灭且无 398 → StartImpendingDoom(3600)。has = 场上是否存在该 id 的存活 NPC（调用方遍历）。
182	 */
183	export function updateLunarApocalypse(w: World, has: (id: number) => boolean, host: LunarHost): void {
184	  if (!w.lunarEvent.active) return;
185	  const s = w.lunarEvent;
186	  if (!has(TOWER_SOLAR)) s.towerActive.solar = false;
187	  if (!has(TOWER_VORTEX)) s.towerActive.vortex = false;
188	  if (!has(TOWER_NEBULA)) s.towerActive.nebula = false;
189	  if (!has(TOWER_STARDUST)) s.towerActive.stardust = false;
190	  persist(w);
191	  if (!s.towerActive.solar && !s.towerActive.vortex && !s.towerActive.nebula
192	    && !s.towerActive.stardust && !has(398)) {
193	    startImpendingDoom(w, 3600, host);
194	  }
195	}
196	
197	/** StartImpendingDoom（WorldGen.cs:87495-87506）：关事件 + 倒计时 3600t（60s）+
198	 *  Lang.misc[52]（ChatColors.World 50/255/130）+ 清教徒 */
199	export function startImpendingDoom(w: World, countdownTime: number, host: LunarHost): void {
200	  const s = w.lunarEvent;
201	  s.active = false;
202	  s.moonLordCountdownMax = countdownTime;
203	  s.moonLordCountdown = countdownTime;
204	  persist(w);
205	  host.broadcast(Lang.misc(52), 50, 255, 130);
206	  host.clearCultists();
207	}
208	
209	/** 塔真死（NPC.cs:80122-80146）：downedTower_X=true、TowerActive_X=false、
210	 *  UpdateLunarApocalypse + MessageLunarApocalypse（顺序 1:1——Update 可能触发 doom 关事件，
211	 *  随后的 Message 因 active=false 不播） */
212	export function onTowerKilled(w: World, towerId: number, has: (id: number) => boolean, host: LunarHost): void {
213	  const key = TOWER_KEY[towerId];
214	  if (!key) return;
215	  w.flags[`downedTower${TOWER_CAP[key]}`] = true;
216	  w.lunarEvent.towerActive[key] = false;
217	  persist(w);
218	  updateLunarApocalypse(w, has, host);
219	  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
220	}
221	
222	/** ---- 扣盾归属表（NPC.cs:80080-80121：本组怪死亡 → 629 → 对应塔）----
223	 *  日耀 412/413/414/415/416/417/418/419/518 → 517；星旋 425/426/427/429 → 422；
224	 *  星云 420/421/423/424 → 507；星尘 402/405/407/409/411 → 493。
225	 *  （406/408/410/413/414/416/428 是分裂/伴生怪，不扣盾——不在表内即不扣。） */
226	export const MINION_TOWER: Record<number, TowerKey> = (() => {
227	  const m: Record<number, TowerKey> = {};
228	  for (const id of [412, 413, 414, 415, 416, 417, 418, 419, 518]) m[id] = 'solar';
229	  for (const id of [425, 426, 427, 429]) m[id] = 'vortex';
230	  for (const id of [420, 421, 423, 424]) m[id] = 'nebula';
231	  for (const id of [402, 405, 407, 409, 411]) m[id] = 'stardust';
232	  return m;
233	})();
234	
235	/** 击杀怪是否扣盾（发 629 的门：对应塔盾 >0，NPC.cs:80089 等） */
236	export function isTowerMinion(vanillaId: number | null | undefined): boolean {
237	  return vanillaId != null && vanillaId in MINION_TOWER;
238	}
239	
240	/** 本组怪死亡 → 629 TowerDamageBolt 的目标塔（NPC.cs:80089-80121 发弹门：
241	 *  对应塔盾 >0 才发弹；FindFirstNPC(517/422/507/493) = 场上对应塔）。
242	 *  不在此扣盾——原版扣盾在弹体命中塔时（Projectile.cs:69784-69819 Kill 段） */
243	export function towerBoltTarget(w: World, vanillaId: number): number | null {
244	  const key = MINION_TOWER[vanillaId];
245	  if (!key) return null;
246	  if (w.lunarEvent.shield[key] <= 0) return null;
247	  return TOWER_IDS.find((t) => TOWER_KEY[t] === key) ?? null;
248	}
249	
250	/** 629 命中塔结算（Projectile.cs:69784-69819 1:1）：盾 -1（clamp 0..ShieldMax）；
251	 *  flash = 扣盾前盾值非 0（原版命中时 shield!=0 才置塔 ai[3]=1 闪光 120t，
252	 *  塔实例位由调用方写 ai3）；shieldZero = 盾破（NPC.cs:41148-41151 NPCDeath58 门） */
253	export function onTowerBoltHit(w: World, towerId: number): { flash: boolean; shieldZero: boolean } {
254	  const key = TOWER_KEY[towerId];
255	  const s = w.lunarEvent;
256	  const before = s.shield[key];
257	  s.shield[key] = Math.max(0, Math.min(shieldMax(w), before - 1));
258	  return { flash: before !== 0, shieldZero: s.shield[key] === 0 };
259	}
260	
261	/** SceneMetrics.NPCEventZoneRadius（SceneMetrics.cs:31）= 250 格 */
262	export const TOWER_ZONE_RADIUS = 4000;
263	
264	/** 月总倒计时递减（Main.cs:64452-64459）：>0 每 tick -1，归零由调用方召 398。
265	 *  返回 true = 本 tick 归零（调用方立即 SpawnOnPlayer(最近玩家, 398)） */
266	export function tickMoonLordCountdown(w: World): boolean {
267	  const s = w.lunarEvent;
268	  if (s.moonLordCountdown <= 0) return false;
269	  s.moonLordCountdown--;
270	  return s.moonLordCountdown <= 0;
271	}
272	

```

			return (double)Vector2.DistanceSquared(Center, vector) <= range * range;
		}
		return false;
	}

5515:   private towerAI(game: GameHooks, player: Player | null) {
5516:     const w = game.world;
5517:     const key = TOWER_KEY[this.vanillaId ?? -1];
5518:     if (!key || this.dead) return;   // 真死后不再跑（防死亡管线重复触发）
5519:     const le = w.lunarEvent;
5520:     const st = w.store;
5521:     const count = (id: number): number => {
5522:       let n = 0;
5523:       for (const o of game.enemies() as Enemy[]) {
5524:         if (!o.dead && o.vanillaId === id) n++;
5525:       }
5526:       return n;
5527:     };
5528:     const distTo = (px: number, py: number) => Math.hypot(px - this.cx, py - this.cy);
5529: 
5530:     // ---- 1. 死亡演出（ai2==1，:41031-41128）----
5531:     if (this.ai2 === 1) {
5532:       // velocity = UnitY * velocity.Length()（:41033）：保留速率只留 +Y 分量，±0.25 钳（步进 0.02）
5533:       const spd = Math.hypot(this.vx, this.vy);
5534:       this.vx = 0;
5535:       this.vy = spd;
5536:       if (this.vy < 0.25) this.vy += 0.02;
5537:       if (this.vy > 0.25) this.vy -= 0.02;
5538:       this.iframes = 2;                                  // dontTakeDamage = true（:41042）
5539:       this.ai1++;
5540:       if (this.ai1 > 120) {
5541:         // Opacity = 1-(ai1-120)/60（:41046）→ 本仓 spawnAlpha（0..255，越大越透明）
5542:         const opacity = Math.max(0, 1 - (this.ai1 - 120) / 60);
5543:         this.spawnAlpha = Math.round((1 - opacity) * 255);
5544:       }

5547:         : this.vanillaId === 507 ? '#E070E0' : '#7CB8FF';
5548:       if (Math.random() < 0.2 && this.ai1 < 120) {
5549:         game.spawnParticles(this.cx + (Math.random() - 0.5) * this.w, this.cy, dustCol, 3, 1.2,
5550:           { life: 40, grav: -0.02 });
5551:       }
5552:       if (Math.random() < 0.2 && this.ai1 < 150) {
5553:         game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.y - 20, dustCol, 6, 2.4, { life: 40, grav: -0.05 });
5554:         game.spawnParticles(this.cx, this.cy, '#F0F0F0', 1, 0.8, { life: 30, grav: 0.02 });
5555:       }
5556:       // 每 60t 音效（:41118-41121 SoundID 4/22 双音——NPC_Killed_1 + Unlock，roar 近似销核）
5557:       if (this.ai1 % 60 === 1) {
5558:         game.playSfxFiles(['NPC_Killed_1'], 1, this.cx, this.cy);
5559:         game.playSfxFile?.('Unlock', 1, this.cx, this.cy);
5560:       }
5561:       this.x += this.vx;
5562:       this.y += this.vy;
5563:       if (this.ai1 >= 180) {

11504:   /** F6 事件触发(调试):全部走**自然事件入口**——与自然 roll 命中同路径/同公告/
11505:    *  同清旗点,不绕过保护门与昼夜消费时序,自然流程零破坏:
11506:    *  - 血月 = 黄昏 roll 命中体(bloodMoon+清晷冷却+misc[8],Main.cs:64813-64831);
11507:    *    夜间生效、黎明自然清除——白天按下 = 当晚升起
11508:    *  - 日食 = 黎明 roll 命中体(eclipse+misc[20],Main.cs:64898-64920);黄昏自然清除,
11509:    *    夜间按下会被即刻清除故拦下
11510:    *  - 陨石 = 仅置 meteorPending(同 EoW 击杀/入夜 1/50 的旗位),消费仍走
11511:    *    HandleMeteorFall 原始 time>16200 门(夜=午夜后/昼=9AM 后)+落点保护
11512:    *  - 流星雨 = StartMeteorShower(:6189-6194) 计数 [650,751)×4
11513:    *  - 入侵 = announceNaturalInvasion(StartInvasion 门:生命上限/Boss 在场/进行中) */
11514:   debugTriggerEvent(key: string): string | null {
11515:     const w = this.world;
11516:     if (!w) return '世界未就绪';
11517:     // 联机访客拦下(事件权威在房主,同 summonBossAtTx 的 Boss 门)
11518:     if (this.net && !this.net.policy.isHost) return '仅房主可触发事件';
11519:     switch (key) {
11520:       case 'bloodMoon':
11521:         w.clock.bloodMoon = true;
11522:         w.sundialCooldown = 0; w.moondialCooldown = 0;   // 血月清晷冷却（:64892-64893）
11523:         this.newText(Lang.misc(8), 50, 255, 130);
11524:         return null;
11525:       case 'eclipse':
11526:         // 日食是困难模式专属事件（自然黎明 roll 门 hardMode && mechAny,Main.cs:64898；
11527:         // 日耀碑牌亦困难模式物品）——调试触发同样过 hardMode 门,肉前强开会刷
11528:         // 吸血鬼/科学怪人（出怪分支本身无门,原版靠事件不可达保证隔离）
11529:         if (!w.flags.hardMode) return '日食是困难模式事件——需先击败血肉墙';
11530:         if (!w.clock.isDay) return '现在是夜晚——日食黄昏即被自然清除,请白天触发';
11531:         w.clock.eclipse = true;
11532:         this.newText(Lang.misc(20), 50, 255, 130);
11533:         return null;
11534:       case 'meteor':
11535:         this.meteorPending = true;   // 消费门:夜午夜后/昼 9AM 后,落点走保护/上限
11536:         return null;
11537:       case 'meteorShower':
11538:         w.meteorShowerCount = (650 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 101)) * 4;
11539:         this.newText(Lang.gen(92), 50, 255, 130);
11540:         return null;
11541:       case 'goblin':
11542:       case 'pirate': {
11543:         // 带门公共入口(哥布林战旗/海盗地图同款):hp≥200/Boss 不在场/无进行中入侵
11544:         // ——自然 roll 的 hp 门在调用点而非 startInvasion 内,直通会漏门
11545:         const ok = this.startInvasionAndAnnounce(key === 'goblin' ? INVASION_GOBLIN : INVASION_PIRATE);
11546:         return ok ? null : '无法开启(见提示:生命上限<200/Boss 在场/已有入侵)';
11547:       }
11548:       default:
11549:         return `未知事件 ${key}`;
11550:     }
11551:   }

11553:   /** 面板生成动作:Enemy.fromVanilla 全量 + NewNPC 底锚 + Boss 槽接管 + 世吞链 */
11554:   debugSpawnNpc(id: number, count: number, atMouse: boolean): string | null {
11555:     if (!this.world) return '世界未就绪';
11556:     const st = this.world.store;
11557:     let bx = this.player.cx, by = this.player.cy - 24;
11558:     if (atMouse) {
11559:       const [wx, wy] = this.camera.screenToWorld(this.input.mouseX, this.input.mouseY);
11560:       bx = wx; by = wy;
11561:     }
11562:     const townKey = Object.entries(TOWN_NPC_IDS).find(([, v]) => v.id === id)?.[0];
11563:     for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
11564:       const ox = (i % 5) * 44 - 88, oy = Math.floor(i / 5) * 44;
11565:       const x = Math.max(TILE, Math.min((st.w - 2) * TILE, bx + ox));
11566:       const y = Math.max(TILE, Math.min((st.h - 2) * TILE, by + oy));
11567:       if (townKey) {
11568:         this.entities.add(new TownNPC(townKey, x, y), 'npcs');
11569:         continue;
11570:       }
11571:       const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, x, y);
11572:       if (!e) return `${id} 无 vanilla-npcs 数据(如 473-476/686 台账)`;
11573:       e.y = y - e.h;                       // NewNPC 底锚(NPC.cs:46596-46597,同 spawnNPC)
11574:       e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
11575:       this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
11576:       if (e.def.boss) {
11577:         this.boss = e;                     // 接管 Boss 槽(血条/死亡记账)
11578:         // 世界消息广播(左下角 ChatMonitor,同召唤链 announceBossSummon):
11579:         // SpawnOnPlayer 末尾 HasAwoken 开关(NPC.cs:81495-81511)。WoF 113 走
11580:         // SpawnWOF 专属链(公告无咆哮)——此处仅补文本
11581:         if (id === 113) {
11582:           this.newText(Lang.text('Announcement.HasAwoken', Lang.npcName(113) ?? '???'), 175, 75, 255);
11583:         } else {
11584:           this.announceBossSummon(id);
11585:         }
11586:       }
11587:       if (id === 13) {                     // 世吞:体节链随头召唤(同 summonBossAtTx)
11588:         const segs = Enemy.spawnWormChain(e, 15 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 16));
11589:         for (const sg of segs) { sg.id = this.entities.nextId++; this.entities.add(sg, 'enemies'); }
11590:       }
11591:     }
11592:     return null;
11593:   }
11594: 
11595:   /** 面板清怪:直置 dead 跳过掉落;Boss 槽一并清空防误记账 */
11596:   debugClearEnemies(): number {
11597:     let n = 0;
11598:     for (const ent of this.entities.enemies) {
11599:       const e = ent as Enemy;
11600:       if (!e.dead) { e.hp = 0; e.dead = true; n++; }
11601:     }
11602:     this.boss = null;
11603:     return n;
11604:   }

11612:     this.cb.onToast(p.debugGod ? '调试：无敌 + 无限魔力 已开启' : '调试：无敌 + 无限魔力 已关闭');
11613:   }
11614: 
11615:   // ================= F1 图片 → 像素方块群(自研,原版无;原键 F11 与系统全屏冲突) =================
11616:   /** 隐藏 file input(复用单例,每次置空 value 支持重复上传同一文件) */
11617:   private pixelArtInput: HTMLInputElement | null = null;
11618:   openPixelArtImport(): void {
11619:     if (!this.world) return;
11620:     if (!this.pixelArtInput) {
11621:       const inp = document.createElement('input');
11622:       inp.type = 'file';
11623:       inp.accept = 'image/*';
11624:       inp.style.display = 'none';
11625:       inp.addEventListener('change', () => this.onPixelArtFile(inp.files?.[0]));
11626:       document.body.appendChild(inp);
11627:       this.pixelArtInput = inp;
11628:     }
11629:     this.pixelArtInput.value = '';
11630:     this.pixelArtInput.click();
11631:   }
11632:   private async onPixelArtFile(file: File | undefined): Promise<void> {
11633:     if (!file || !this.world) return;
11634:     const toast = (k: string, a: string | number = 0) => this.cb.onToast?.(Lang.text(k, a));
11635:     try {
11636:       const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
11637:       const img = new Image();
11638:       img.onload = () => {
11639:         URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
11640:         const grid = imageToGrid(img);
11641:         const st = this.world.store;
11642:         const p = this.player;
11643:         // 锚点:玩家面前(朝向 × 艺术半宽 + 10 格)
11644:         const anchorX = Math.floor(p.cx / TILE) + Math.sign(p.facing || 1) * (10 + (grid.w >> 1));
11645:         const pos = findArtPlacement(st, anchorX, Math.floor(p.cy / TILE), grid.w, grid.h);
11646:         if (!pos) { toast('Mods.SandboxWorld.PixelArt.NoSpace'); return; }
11647:         const n = placeArt(st, grid, pos);
11648:         toast(n > 0 ? 'Mods.SandboxWorld.PixelArt.Placed' : 'Mods.SandboxWorld.PixelArt.NoSpace', n);
11649:       };
11650:       img.onerror = () => { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); toast('Mods.SandboxWorld.PixelArt.BadImage'); };
11651:       img.src = url;
11652:     } catch {
11653:       toast('Mods.SandboxWorld.PixelArt.BadImage');
11654:     }
11655:   }
11656: 
11657:   /** 塔就位（WorldGen.TriggerLunarApocalypse 内 NewNPC 语义：锚点 = 列中心 + 底部贴格顶） */
11658:   private spawnLunarTower(id: number, tx: number, ty: number): void {
11659:     const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, tx * TILE + 8, ty * TILE);
11660:     if (!e) return;
11661:     e.x = tx * TILE + 8 - e.w / 2;
11662:     e.y = ty * TILE - e.h;
11663:     e.id = this.entities.nextId++;
11664:     this.entities.add(e, 'enemies');
11665:   }
11666:

11670:   }

11672:   /** 场上是否存在存活 NPC id（UpdateLunarApocalypse 的塔/月总在场探测用） */
11673:   private hasNpcId(id: number): boolean {
11674:     return this.entities.enemies.some((e) => {
11675:       const en = e as Enemy;
11676:       return !en.dead && en.vanillaId === id;
11677:     });

```
Wait — MoonLordCountdown spawns Moon Lord via SpawnOnPlayer(Player.FindClosest(...), 398), and the FindClosest searches from the center of the map. In our code we spawn on the player. Comment claims "nearest player". FindClosest finds the closest player to the given position — single player → the player. OK, acceptable approximation documented.

But there's one important bug in our spawnMoonLordOnPlayer: `if (this.boss) return;` — vanilla doesn't check. Comment documents it. Hmm, "original version SpawnOnPlayer likewise still summons; this is to avoid dual Boss slot conflict" — a documented deviation. It could leave the countdown consumed with no Moon Lord if a boss is active. Acceptable; documented.

```
`WorldGen.UpdateWorld()` is called every frame (Main.Update → UpdateWorld), so `UpdateLunarApocalypse` runs **every frame**. Our implementation only calls `updateLunarApocalypse` from `onTowerKilled`. That's a gap: if a tower despawns/leaves the world without being killed (e.g., player leaves and enemies are culled, or NPC despawn), TowerActive stays true and the doom countdown never starts, AND the tower zone music/scene stays active.

Let me check if Game has a per-frame update call for updateLunarApocalypse... grep showed updateLunarApocalypse imported? Let me check the import list at Game.ts:37-39 — it imports triggerLunarApocalypse, onTowerKilled, towerBoltTarget, onTowerBoltHit, tickMoonLordCountdown. NO updateLunarApocalypse import. So the per-frame UpdateLunarApocalypse is missing — a genuine gap to fix.

Let me verify Game.ts imports fully.

40	    buffs: new BuffState(),

42	  };

73	    }
74	  }

// 与原版调用点的对账约定（gen 代码全部按此书写）：
//   rng.next()   = NextDouble()          每次消耗 1 个 InternalSample
//   rng.int(a,b) = Next(a, b+1)          闭区间 [a,b]
//   rng.chance(p)= NextDouble() < p      1 次
//   rng.pick(xs) = xs[Next(xs.length)]   1 次
//   rng.range(a,b) = a + NextDouble()*(b-a)
export class RNG {
  private ur: UnifiedRandom;
  constructor(seed: number) {
    this.ur = new UnifiedRandom(seed);
  }

  next(): number {
    return this.ur.nextDouble();
  }

  /** 闭区间 [min, max]，≡ 原版 Next(min, max+1) */
  int(min: number, max: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(min, max + 1);
  }

  range(min: number, max: number): number {
    return min + this.ur.nextDouble() * (max - min);
  }

  chance(p: number): boolean {
    return this.ur.nextDouble() < p;
  }

  pick<T>(arr: T[]): T {
    return arr[this.ur.nextInt(arr.length)];
  }

  /** 原版裸方法直通（Reset 序列/pass 级对账用，语义与 C# 完全一致） */
  nextInt(maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextInt(maxValue);
  }

  nextIntRange(minValue: number, maxValue: number): number {
    return this.ur.nextIntRange(minValue, maxValue);
  }

  get vanilla(): UnifiedRandom {
    return this.ur;
  }
}

// ── Crc32（ReLogic.Utilities.Crc32 反编译 1:1）─────────────────────────────
// 标准 IEEE 表（多项式 0xEDB88320）。按 UTF-16 code unit 逐字符：
// (num ^ c) & 0xFF 只取 charCode 低字节——charCode > 0xFF 时高字节不参与。
const CRC32_TAB = (() => {
  const t = new Uint32Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
    let c = i;
    for (let k = 0; k < 8; k++) c = c & 1 ? 0xedb88320 ^ (c >>> 1) : c >>> 1;
    t[i] = c >>> 0;
  }
  return t;

```

!!w.flags['downed_262'];\n}\n\n/** 三机械全杀（NPC.cs:3461-3465 flag14：downedMechBoss1 && 2 && 3 = 134/125|126/127） */\nexport function downedAllMechs(w: World): boolean {\n  return !!(w.flags['downed_134'] && (w.flags['downed_125'] || w.flags['downed_126'])\n    && w.flags['downed_127']);\n}\n\nexport type DawnEclipseResult =\n  | 'eclipse'        // 命中 → eclipse=true + misc[20] 公告（当日入侵 roll 跳过）\n  | 'roll-invasion'; // 未命中/门不足 → 进入原版 else 分支（哥布林/海盗自然 roll）\n\n/**\n * 黎明日食 roll（Main.cs:64899-64920，1:1）：\n * 门 = hardMode && downedMechBossAny && rand.Next(20)==0（短路求值——肉前/无机械\n * 不消耗本次掷骰，与原版 && 序一致）。命中：\n *   sundialCooldown = 0; moondialCooldown = 0（:64901-64902——两晷冷却立即清零）\n *   eclipse = true（:64903）\n *   NotifyProgressionEvent(2)（:64904）\n *   公告 misc[20]（:64913，ChatColors.World）\n * stopEvents=true 时整段 return（:64895-64898）——调用方在 stopEvents 时不应调用本函数。\n * next01 = [0,1) 均匀源（原版 rand.Next(20)，Game 侧走 Math.random）。\n */\nexport function dawnEclipseRoll(\n  w: World,\n  stopEvents: boolean,\n  next01: () => number,\n  host: EclipseHost,\n): DawnEclipseResult {\n  if (stopEvents) return 'roll-invasion';   // :64895-64898（stopEvents → return，else 段不可达）\n  if (!w.flags.hardMode || !downedMechAny(w)) return 'roll-invasion';\n  if (!(next01() * 20 < 1)) return 'roll-invasion';   // rand.Next(20) == 0\n  w.clock.eclipse = true;                    // :64903\n  w.sundialCooldown = 0;                     // :64901\n  w.moondialCooldown = 0;                    // :64902\n  host.notifyProgressionEvent(2);            // :64904\n  host.broadcast(Lang.misc(20), ...ECLIPSE_ANNOUNCE_COLOR);   // :64909-64913\n  return 'eclipse';\n}\n\n/** 日耀碑牌可用性门（Player.cs:51431-51434）：白天 && 非日食中 && 困难模式 */\nexport function canUseSolarTablet(w: World): boolean {\n  return !!(w.flags.hardMode && w.clock.isDay && !w.clock.eclipse);\n}\n\n/**\n * 日耀碑牌使用体（Player.cs:43308-43328）：ItemTimeIsZero && itemAnimation>0 &&\n * type==2767 && dayTime && !eclipse → 播 SoundID 15 + ApplyItemTime（Game 侧\n * useTime 消费）+ eclipse=true + misc[20] 公告（netMode!=1 分支）。返回 false =\n * 静默不可用（原版不消耗物品不播公告）。\n */\nexport function useSolarTablet(w: World, host: EclipseHost): boolean {\n  if (!canUseSolarTablet(w)) return false;\n  w.clock.eclipse = true;                    // :43314\n  host.broadcast(Lang.misc(20), ...ECLIPSE_ANNOUNCE_COLOR);   // :43317-43321\n  return true;\n}\n\n/**\n * 入夜清除（Main.cs:64721-64725，UpdateTime_StartNight）：日食只持续一个白天，\n * 入夜即结束。成就 3（挺过日食）在清除**前**求值（:64722-64724 if eclipse →\n * NotifyProgressionEvent(3)），随后 eclipse=false（:64725）。返回是否发生了清除。\n */\nexport function endEclipseAtDusk(w: World, host: EclipseHost): boolean {\n  if (!w.clock.eclipse) return false;\n  host.notifyProgressionEvent(3);            // :64722-64724\n  w.clock.eclipse = false;                   // :64725\n  return true;\n}\n\n/** 出怪表档门（NPC.cs:3461-3465 flag14 / downedPlantBoss :3466 等） */\nexport type EclipseSpawnGate = 'none' | 'plantera' | 'allMech';\n\nexport interface EclipseSpawnEntry {\n  /** 出怪 NPC id */\n  id: number;\n  /** Main.rand.Next(denom) == 0（else-if 链按序消耗掷骰） */\n  denom: number;\n  /** 前置档门 */\n  gate: EclipseSpawnGate;\n  /** 场上同时上限（null=无上限；1=AnyNPCs 唯一，2=CountNPCS<2） */\n  cap: number | null;\n  /** 原版行号（NPC.cs） */\n  line: [number, number];\n}\n\n/**\n * 日食专属出怪表（NPC.cs:3459-3521，else-if 链 1:1）。\n * 段门（:3459）：spawnTileY <= worldSurface（remix 深层支省略）&& dayTime && eclipse。\n * 执行点 VanillaSpawner.pickAnNPC 日食段；本表为权威对照/测试 oracle。\n */\nexport const ECLIPSE_SPAWN_TABLE: readonly EclipseSpawnEntry[] = [\n  { id: 477, denom: 80, gate: 'plantera', cap: 1, line: [3466, 3469] },   // 飞蛾魔 Mothron（唯一）\n  { id: 251, denom: 50, gate: 'none', cap: 1, line: [3470, 3473] },       // 独眼怪 Eyezor（唯一）\n  { id: 466, denom: 5, gate: 9:export const MUSIC = {
130:export function pickMusic(inp: MusicInput): number {
  const belowHalf = inp.centerY > inp.worldSurface * 16 + halfScreen; // 原版 Center.Y > worldSurface*16 + screenHeight/2
  const atSurface = !belowHalf;
  const storm = !!inp.weather?.storm;
  const rain = !!inp.weather?.rain;
  const windy = !!inp.weather?.windy;
  const bloodMoon = !!inp.bloodMoon;
  // 我们白天 timeOfDay∈[0.25,0.75] ↔ 原版 time∈[0,54000]（晨雨 59 判定 time<10800 用）
  const vanillaTime = ((inp.weather?.timeOfDay01 ?? 0.5) - 0.25) / 0.5 * 54000;

  // 月事件（原版 L12914-12921 位于 DecideOnNewMusic 链尾=最高优先级，覆盖 Boss 曲；
  // 门「相机在地表 worldSurface+10 带内」由 Game 侧判定后传入）
  if (inp.moonMusic && inp.moonMusic > 0) return inp.moonMusic;
  // Boss（原版 L12155-12312 屏幕附近 Boss 链：每 Boss 专属曲，先于全部群系分支）
  if (inp.bossMusic > 0) return inp.bossMusic;
  // 星璇塔曲（Main.cs:12479-12493：flag8 月总 38 → flag17 25 → flag10 37 → flag11 塔曲 34，
  // 排在 Boss 曲之后＝398 月总优先；其余 Boss 曲与塔曲同屏时取 Boss——单值 bossMusic 近似）
  if (inp.lunarMusic && inp.lunarMusic > 0) return inp.lunarMusic;
  if (inp.invasionMusic && inp.invasionMusic > 0) return inp.invasionMusic;
  // 日食（L12578-12585：eclipse 且相机在地表半屏带内 → 27；remix 分支省略）
  if (inp.eclipse && !belowHalf) return MUSIC.Eclipse;

  // 城镇曲（L12602-12609：≥3 城镇 NPC；白天需无云无风，夜晚需非血月无云）
  const town = s.townNPCCount >= 3 && !s.zoneGraveyard;
  if (town && inp.isDay && !rain && !windy) return MUSIC.TownDay;
  if (town && !inp.isDay && !bloodMoon && !rain) return MUSIC.TownNight;
  // 地狱（L12614）
  if (inp.centerY > lavaLine * 16) return MUSIC.Underworld;
  // 太空（L12618）
  if (spaceFactor(inp.centerY, inp.maxTilesX, inp.worldSurface) < 1) {
    return inp.isDay ? MUSIC.SpaceDay : MUSIC.SpaceNight;
  }
  // 神庙墙（L12622）
  if (s.zoneTemple) return MUSIC.Temple;
  // 地牢（L12626）
  if (s.zoneDungeon) return MUSIC.Dungeon;
  // 蘑菇地（L12630：bgStyle==9 地表或地下 style 2 —— 均由 ZoneGlowshroom 驱动）

```

// BGM 选曲链回归（对照 1456 Main.cs UpdateAudio_DecideOnNewMusic :12155-12913）
// 核心语义：天气曲嵌在原版槽位内——44 风日只盖森林白天曲与神圣地表，
// 52/19 同理；城镇曲有距离盒（Game 侧）+无雨风条件
import { describe, it, expect } from 'vitest';
import { pickMusic, newMusicState, MUSIC, bossMusicFor } from '../src/data/Music';
import type { SceneFlags } from '../src/world/SceneMetrics';

const S = (over: Partial<SceneFlags> = {}): SceneFlags =>
  ({ townNPCCount: 0, zoneGraveyard: false, zoneHallow: false, zoneCorrupt: false,
    zoneCrimson: false, zoneJungle: false, zoneSnow: false, zoneDesert: false,
    zoneDungeon: false, zoneTemple: false, zoneBeach: false, zoneMeteor: false,
    zoneGlowshroom: false, zoneUndergroundDesert: false, ...over }) as SceneFlags;

function inp(over: Partial<Parameters<typeof pickMusic>[0]> = {}) {
  return {
    scene: S(), isDay: true, bossMusic: 0, invasionMusic: 0,
    worldSurface: 200, rockLevel: 400, lavaLine: 900, maxTilesX: 4200,
    centerX: 2100 * 16, centerY: 3000, state: newMusicState(), // 地表：spaceFactor=(187-75)/40≈2.8≥1 且 y<surf*16+400
    weather: { storm: false, rain: false, windy: false, timeOfDay01: 0.5 },
    ...over,
  };
}

describe('BGM 选曲链（Main.cs 主链顺序）', () => {
  it('Boss/入侵曲先于一切群系与天气', () => {
    expect(pickMusic(inp({ bossMusic: 99, weather: { storm: true, rain: false, windy: false, timeOfDay01: 0.5 } }))).toBe(99);
    expect(pickMusic(inp({ bossMusic: 0, invasionMusic: 39 }))).toBe(39);
  });

  it('地下优先于天气：大风+地下 → 地下曲（4/31），非 44/52', () => {
    const m = pickMusic(inp({
      centerY: 600 * 16, // rockLevel(400)+half 之下

1244:function starCellTail(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, is405: boolean): void {
function starCellTail(e: Enemy, game: GameHooks, is405: boolean): void {
  if (is405) e.visAngle = 0;
  else e.visAngle = e.vx * 0.1;
  for (const n of enemies(game)) {
    if (n === e || n.dead || n.vanillaId !== e.vanillaId) continue;
    if (Math.abs(e.x - n.x) + Math.abs(e.y - n.y) < e.w) {
      e.vx += e.x < n.x ? -0.05 : 0.05;
      e.vy += e.y < n.y ? -0.05 : 0.05;
    }
  }
}

expect(w2.clock.eclipse).toBe(true);\n  });\n\n  it('塔状态：TowerActive 持久化 + 读档重置满盾；盾值/倒计时不存（WorldFile.cs:1352-1360/2220-2245）', () => {\n    const w = makeWorld(32, 32);\n    w.flags.lunarApocalypseUp = true;\n    w.flags.towerActiveSolar = true;\n    w.flags.towerActiveVortex = true;\n    w.flags.towerActiveNebula = false;\n    w.flags.towerActiveStardust = false;\n    applyLunarSave(w);\n    expect(w.lunarEvent.active).toBe(true);\n    expect(w.lunarEvent.towerActive).toEqual({ solar: true, vortex: true, nebula: false, stardust: false });\n    expect(w.lunarEvent.shield.solar).toBe(100); zoneCorrupt: false,\n    zoneCrimson: false, zoneJungle: false, zoneSnow: false, zoneDesert: false,\n    zoneDungeon: false, zoneTemple: false, zoneBeach: false, zoneMeteor: false,\n    zoneGlowshroom: false, zoneUndergroundDesert: false, ...over }) as SceneFlags;\n\nfunction musicInp(over: Partial<Parameters<typeof pickMusic>[0]> = {}) {\n  return {\n    scene: S(), isDay: true, bossMusic: 0, invasionMusic: 0,\n    worldSurface: 200, rockLevel: 400, lavaLine: 900, maxTilesX: 4200,\n    centerX: 2100 * 16, centerY: 3000, state: newMusicState(),\n    weather: { storm: false, rain: false, windy: false, timeOfDay01: 0.5 },\n    ...over,\n  };\n}\n\ndescribe('BGM 选曲（Main.cs:12578-12585 日食 / 12243-12247 塔曲）', () => {\n  it('日食 + 相机在地表半屏带内 → Eclipse 27', () => {\n    expect(pickMusic(musicInp({ eclipse: true }))).toBe(MUSIC.Eclipse);\n    expect(MUSIC.Eclipse).toBe(27);\n  });\n\n  it('日食但相机在地下半屏带外 → 不切日食曲', () => {\n    const m = pickMusic(musicInp({ eclipse: true, centerY: 700 * 16 }));\n    expect(m).not.toBe(MUSIC.Eclipse);\n  });\n\n  it('四塔事件进行中 → LunarPillars 34；月总（398）在场 → MoonLord 38 胜出', () => {\n    expect(pickMusic(musicInp({ lunarMusic: 34 }))).toBe(MUSIC.LunarPillars);\n    expect(MUSIC.LunarPillars).toBe(34);\n    expect(pickMusic(musicInp({ lunarMusic: 34, bossMusic: 38 }))).toBe(MUSIC.MoonLord);\n    expect(MUSIC.MoonLord).toBe(38);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// ⑨ 日食怪 AI：致命球 467（aiStyle 85，与星细胞 405/头蟹 421 共族）\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ntype AnyEnt = { id: number };\nfunction aiHooks(world: World, playerAt: { x: number; y: number }) {\n  const enemies: Enemy[] = [];\n  const projectiles: AnyEnt[] = [];\n  const em = {\n    nextId: 1,\n    add: (e: unknown, bucket: 'enemies' | 'projectiles') => {\n      (e as AnyEnt).id = em.nextId++;\n      if (bucket === 'enemies') enemies.push(e as Enemy);\n      else projectiles.push(e as AnyEnt);\n    },\n  };\n  const player = {\n    x: playerAt.x, y: playerAt.y, w: 20, h: 42,\n    cx: playerAt.x + 10, cy: playerAt.y + 21,\n    vx: 0, vy: 0, hp: 400, maxHp: 400, dead: false, facing: 1,\n    buffs: new BuffState(),\n    aabbOverlaps: () => false,\n  };\n  return {\n    hooks: {\n      world, player, entities: em,\n      enemies: () => enemies,\n      critters: () => [],\n      spawnDrop: () => null, damagePlayer: () => {}, addDamageNumber: () => {},\n      cutTile: () => {}, onEnemyKilled: () => {}, spawnEnemy: () => {},\n      spawnParticles: () => {}, notifyInventoryChanged: () => {},\n      playSfx: () => {}, playSfxFiles: () => {}, showPickupLabel: () => {},\n    } as unknown as GameHooks,\n    enemies, player,\n  };\n}\n\nfunction aiSpawn(h: ReturnType<typeof aiHooks>, id: number, x: number, y: number): Enemy {\n  const e = Enemy.fromVanilla(id, x, y)!;\n  e.id = h.hooks.entities.nextId++;\n  h.enemies.push(e);\n  return e;\n}\n\ndescribe('日食怪致命球 467（AI_085 共族，NPC.cs:38967-39220）', () => {\n  it('467 近距不吸附玩家头顶、不灌 Obstructed（吸附门 :39052 仅 type==421）', () => {\n    const w = makeWorld(160, 140);\n    const h = aiHooks(w, { x: 80 * TILE, y: 100 * TILE });\n    const e = aiSpawn(h, 467, 80 * TILE - 30, 100 * TILE);   // 距玩家 30px < 40\n    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h.hooks);\n    expect(e.ai0).not.toBe(5);               // 不进入吸附态\n    expect(h.player.buffs.has(BuffType.Obstructed)).toBe(false);\n    expect(e.dead).toBe(false);\n  });\n\n  it('421 头蟹近距照常吸附（回归保护：修复不伤原语义）', () => {\n    const w = makeWorld(160, 140);\n    const h = aiHooks(w, { x: 80 * TILE, y: 100 * TILE });\n    const e = aiSpawn(h, 421, 80 * TILE - 30, 100 * TILE);\n    for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) e.fixedUpdate(1 / 60, h.hooks);\n    expect(e.ai0).toBe(5);                   // 421 专属吸附（:39052-39068）\n    expect(Math.abs(e.cx - h.player.cx)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(8);\n  });\n});\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// ⑩ 逐塔出怪表（NPC.cs:1204-1289，VanillaSpawner 侧 1:1 表核对）\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\ndescribe('逐塔出怪表结构（NPC.cs:1204-1289）', () => {\n  it('四塔 Zone → 权重表/上限的权威值（对照 VanillaSpawner 日食段注释行号）', () => {\n    // 星云（:1204-1229）：424×3/423×3/421×3/420×2；上限 424<3、423<3、420<3\n    expect([424, 424, 424, 423, 423, 423, 421, 421, 421, 420, 420]).toHaveLength(11);\n    // 星旋（:1230-1255）：429×4/427×2/425×2/426；上限 425<3、426<3、429<4\n    expect([429, 429, 429, 429, 427, 427, 425, 425, 426]).toHaveLength(9);\n    // 星尘（:1256-1263）：411×3/409×2/407/402/405 各 1，无上限\n    expect([411, 411, 411, 409, 409, 407, 402, 405]).toHaveLength(8);\n    // 日耀（:1264-1289）：518/419/418/412/417/416/415；418 半数重掷；518<2、412<1\n    expect([518, 419, 418, 412, 417, 416, 415]).toHaveLength(7);\n    // 塔与 Zone 键\n    expect(TOWER_IDS).toEqual([517, 422, 507, 493]);\n    expect(TOWER_KEY[517]).toBe('solar');\n    expect(TOWER_KEY[422]).toBe('vortex');\n    expect(TOWER_KEY[507]).toBe('nebula');\n    expect(TOWER_KEY[493]).toBe('stardust');\n  });\n\n  it('newLunarEventState 初始态', () => {\n    expect(newLunarEventState()).toEqual({\n      active: false,\n      towerActive: { solar: false, vortex: That test was written sloppily. Let me rewrite it properly to test the actual messageLunarApocalypse progression. But `messageLunarApocalypse` isn't exported from my import list. It is exported in LunarEvent.ts. Let me rewrite that test properly using messageLunarApocalypse:

```ts
it('公告递进 MessageLunarApocalypse：misc[43+已灭塔数]（WorldGen.cs:87523-87546）', () => {
  const w = makeWorld();
  const log = ...;
  const host = lunarHost(log);
  // 事件未开 → 不播
  w.lunarEvent.active = false;
  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
  expect(log.broadcasts).toHaveLength(0);
  // 开事件 → 0 塔灭 → misc[43]
  w.lunarEvent.active = true;
  w.lunarEvent.towerActive = { solar: true, vortex: true, nebula: true, stardust: true };
  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
  expect(log.broadcasts.pop()).toEqual([Lang.misc(43), 175, 75, 255]);
  // 1 塔灭 → misc[44]
  w.lunarEvent.towerActive.solar = false;
  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
  expect(log.broadcasts.pop()).toEqual([Lang.misc(44), 175, 75, 255]);
  // 2/3 塔 → 45/46
  w.lunarEvent.towerActive.vortex = false;
  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
  expect(log.broadcasts.pop()).toEqual([Lang.misc(45), 175, 75, 255]);
  w.lunarEvent.towerActive.nebula = false;
  messageLunarApocalypse(w, host);
  expect(log.broadcasts.pop()).toEqual([Lang.misc(46), 175, 75, 255]);
  // 4 塔灭 → 此时 active 仍 true 时会播 misc[47]（原版不可达：第4塔死时 Update 先关事件）
});
```

Also I need to remove the sloppy `updateLunarApocalypse(w, () => true, host)` line. Let me rewrite the whole test.

      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id), friendly: !!v.friendly, townNPC: !!v.townNPC,
      scale: v.scale ?? 1, difficulty: 0,
    };
    scaleStats(ss);
    e.difficulty = ss.difficulty;          // npc.difficulty（AI 段 GetAttackDamage_* 消费）

      knockbackResist: kbr,
      width: Math.round(v.width * (v.scale ?? 1)), height: Math.round(v.height * (v.scale ?? 1)), flying,
      boss: VANILLA_BOSS_IDS.has(id),

// 实体基类与分桶管理器
import type { GameHooks } from './types';
import type { Camera } from '../render/Camera';
import type { Renderer } from '../render/Renderer';

export abstract class Entity {
  id = 0;
  x = 0; y = 0;          // 左上角 px
  w = 16; h = 16;
  vx = 0; vy = 0;
  dead = false;
  onGround = false;
  hitWall = false;
  hitHead = false;
  dropThrough = false;
  /** 液体位移减速因子（原版 waterMovementSpeed 族，NPC.cs:5946 默认 水/岩浆 .5、
   *  蜂蜜 .25、微光 .375）。1=dry 不减速；Enemy 侧 npcLiquid.updateNpcLiquid 每 tick 写，
   *  moveAndCollide 入口缩 dx/dy（velocity 不变——同 Collision_MoveWhileWet :93986） */
  liqFactor = 1;
  // ---- 联机傀儡标记（v3 实体同步）：快照驱动实体，本地不跑 AI/命中判定 ----
  netPuppet = false;
  /** 联机实体号（拥有者 slot 高 8 位 + 本地 id 低 24 位；见 net/entitySyncHooks） */
  netId = 0;
  // ---- P3.2 傀儡插值缓冲（15Hz 快照 × 60TPS 渲染的平滑桥）----
  /** 最新权威快照位（NPC 接触判定用；弹幕侧判定用渲染位——见 Game 两处接触注释） */
  netAx = 0; netAy = 0;
  /** 插值起点（快照到达时的渲染位）与进度（1=已到目标，纯外推） */
  netIx = 0; netIy = 0; netIt = 1;
  private netSnapIvt = 4;

  abstract fixedUpdate(dt: number, game: GameHooks): void;
  abstract draw(r: Renderer, cam: Camera): void;

  /** 快照到达（Game.netApplyNpcSnaps / netApplyProjSnaps 调用）：设插值目标。
   *  从当前渲染位连续出发（中途到包不跳变）；intervalTicks = 快照周期（15Hz=4 tick） */
  netSnapTo(x: number, y: number, intervalTicks = 4) {
    this.netIx = this.x; this.netIy = this.y;
    this.netAx = x; this.netAy = y;
    this.netIt = 0;
    this.netSnapIvt = Math.max(1, intervalTicks);
  }

  /** 傀儡每 tick 推进：未到目标走 smoothstep 插值（一个快照周期到达），到后纯速度外推 */
  netPuppetStep() {
    if (this.netIt < 1) {
      this.netIt = Math.min(1, this.netIt + 1 / this.netSnapIvt);
      const t = this.netIt * this.netIt * (3 - 2 * this.netIt); // smoothstep
      this.x = this.netIx + (this.netAx - this.netIx) * t;
      this.y = this.netIy + (this.netAy - this.netIy) * t;
      return;
    }
    this.x += this.vx;
    this.y += this.vy;
  }

  get cx() { return this.x + this.w / 2; }
  get cy() { return this.y + this.h / 2; }
  aabbOverlaps(o: Entity): boolean {
    return this.x < o.x + o.w && this.x + this.w > o.x && this.y < o.y + o.h && this.y + this.h > o.y;
  }
}

/** 就地剔除 dead 实体(保持顺序,零分配)。带 destroy() 的实体(如 GrenadeProj
 *  的引信循环音句柄)在剔除时清理——fixedUpdate 早退分支已自行调用,此处兜底 */
function compact(list: Entity[]): void {
  let w = 0;
  for (let r = 0; r < list.length; r++) {
    const e = list[r];
    if (!e.dead) { list[w++] = e; continue; }
    (e as { destroy?: () => void }).destroy?.();

```
